    var $firstImage = $('#first-image-comp_00005BD2'),
        images = $firstImage.data('image'),
        keys = Object.keys(images).reverse(),
        length = keys.length;

    for(var i = 0; i < length; i++) {
        if(keys[i] !== IPXL.initialBreakpoint) {
            continue;
        } else {
            break;
        }
    }

    for(i = i; i < length; i++) {
        if(images[keys[i]]) {
            $firstImage.prop('src', images[keys[i]]);
            break;
        }
    }

    var inspi_aantal = $(".inspi_block").length;

    /*DISPLAY ARROWS*/
    $(document).ready(function() {
        var inspi_breed_arrow = $(window).width();

        /*Desktop*/
        if (inspi_breed_arrow > 1024) {
            if (inspi_aantal < 4) {
                $("#inspi_prev").css("display", "none");
                $("#inspi_next").css("display", "none");
            }
        }

        /*Mobile*/
        else if (inspi_breed_arrow < 660) {
            if (inspi_aantal < 1) {
                $("#inspi_prev").css("display", "none");
                $("#inspi_next").css("display", "none");
            }
        }

        /*Tablet*/
        else {
            if (inspi_aantal < 3) {
                $("#inspi_prev").css("display", "none");
                $("#inspi_next").css("display", "none");
            }
        }
    });

    /*PREV ARROW*/
    function PrevInspi() {
        var inspi_margin = $("#inspi_inner").css("marginLeft");
        var inspi_margin_value = parseInt(inspi_margin, 10);
        if (inspi_margin_value < 0) {
            $("#inspi_next").css("color", "#d5d6d2");
            $("#inspi_inner").animate({
                "marginLeft": "+=300px"
            });
        } else {
            $("#inspi_prev").css("color", "#f2f3f1");
        }
    }

    /*NEXT ARROW*/
    function NextInspi() {
        var inspi_margin = $("#inspi_inner").css("marginLeft");
        var inspi_margin_value = parseInt(inspi_margin, 10);
        var inspi_breed = $(window).width();

        /*Desktop*/
        if (inspi_breed > 1024) {
            var inspi_max = -((parseInt(inspi_aantal) * 300) - 900);
        }

        /*Mobile*/
        else if (inspi_breed < 660) {
            var inspi_max = -((parseInt(inspi_aantal) * 300) - 300);
        }

        /*Tablet*/
        else {
            var inspi_max = -((parseInt(inspi_aantal) * 300) - 600);
        }

        if (inspi_margin_value > inspi_max) {
            $("#inspi_inner").animate({
                "marginLeft": "-=300px"
            });
            $("#inspi_prev").css("color", "#d5d6d2");
        } else {
            $("#inspi_next").css("color", "#f2f3f1");
        }
    }

</script>
